{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nUnified n=500 analysis for phase-gated compound sparsity (PACS).\\n\\nAnalyses performed:\\n  1. Bonferroni correction on all reported p-values\\n\\nInput:  profiling_output_{n500_,}bf16_{ds,qw}_{imp,rand}_s{015..030}/profiling_results.json\\nOutput: analysis_output/n500_unified{_dryrun}.json\\n        analysis_output/n500_unified{_dryrun}.csv  (--csv)\\n        LaTeX table rows to stdout (--latex)\\n\\nConstraints:\\n  D054: median only, no mean\\n  D059: d_z within-importance only; imp-vs-rand gets Wilcoxon + median_diff\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport json\\nimport argparse\\nimport sys\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\nfrom typing import Optional, List, Dict, Tuple, Any\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\nfrom scipy import stats\\n\\nBASE = Path(\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/pacs\\\")\\n\\nMODEL_MAP = {\\\"deepseek\\\": \\\"ds\\\", \\\"qwen\\\": \\\"qw\\\"}\\nMASKING_TYPES = [\\\"imp\\\", \\\"rand\\\"]\\nSPARSITY_LABELS = [\\\"s015\\\", \\\"s020\\\", \\\"s025\\\", \\\"s030\\\"]\\nSPARSITY_VALUES = {\\\"s015\\\": 0.15, \\\"s020\\\": 0.20, \\\"s025\\\": 0.25, \\\"s030\\\": 0.30}\\nSPARSITY_KEY_MAP = {\\\"s015\\\": \\\"0.15\\\", \\\"s020\\\": \\\"0.2\\\", \\\"s025\\\": \\\"0.25\\\", \\\"s030\\\": \\\"0.3\\\"}\\n\\nWITHIN_IMP_PAIRS = [\\n    (\\\"s015\\\", \\\"s020\\\"),\\n    (\\\"s020\\\", \\\"s025\\\"),\\n    (\\\"s025\\\", \\\"s030\\\"),\\n    (\\\"s015\\\", \\\"s030\\\"),\\n]\\n\\nB_BOOT = 10000\\nSEED = 42\\nALPHA = 0.05\\nN_PERM = 5000\\n\\n\\n# ── Path / IO ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\\n\\ndef dir_name(model_short: str, masking: str, slabel: str, n500: bool = True) -> str:\\n    prefix = \\\"n500_\\\" if n500 else \\\"\\\"\\n    return f\\\"profiling_output_{prefix}bf16_{model_short}_{masking}_{slabel}\\\"\\n\\n\\ndef load_problems(dirpath: Path) -> Optional[List[dict]]:\\n    fpath = dirpath / \\\"profiling_results.json\\\"\\n    if not fpath.exists():\\n        return None\\n    with open(fpath) as f:\\n        data = json.load(f)\\n    ppi = data.get(\\\"per_problem_ii\\\", {})\\n    keys = list(ppi.keys())\\n    if not keys:\\n        return None\\n    return ppi[keys[0]]\\n\\n\\n# ── Statistics ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\\n\\ndef extract_diffs(problems: List[dict], pa: str = \\\"low\\\", pb: str = \\\"high\\\") -> np.ndarray:\\n    diffs = []\\n    for p in problems:\\n        va, vb = p.get(f\\\"ii_{pa}\\\"), p.get(f\\\"ii_{pb}\\\")\\n        if va is not None and vb is not None:\\n            diffs.append(va - vb)\\n    return np.array(diffs)\\n\\n\\ndef bootstrap_median_ci(arr: np.ndarray, B: int = B_BOOT,\\n                        alpha: float = ALPHA, seed: int = SEED) -> Tuple[float, float]:\\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\\n    n = len(arr)\\n    medians = np.empty(B)\\n    for i in range(B):\\n        medians[i] = np.median(arr[rng.randint(0, n, size=n)])\\n    lo = float(np.percentile(medians, 100 * alpha / 2))\\n    hi = float(np.percentile(medians, 100 * (1 - alpha / 2)))\\n    return lo, hi\\n\\n\\ndef paired_cohens_dz(diffs: np.ndarray) -> float:\\n    if len(diffs) < 2:\\n        return float(\\\"nan\\\")\\n    s = np.std(diffs, ddof=1)\\n    if s == 0:\\n        return float(\\\"nan\\\")\\n    return float(np.mean(diffs) / s)\\n\\n\\ndef bootstrap_dz_ci(diffs: np.ndarray, B: int = B_BOOT,\\n                     alpha: float = ALPHA, seed: int = SEED) -> Tuple[float, float]:\\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\\n    n = len(diffs)\\n    boot = np.empty(B)\\n    for i in range(B):\\n        sample = diffs[rng.randint(0, n, size=n)]\\n        s = sample.std(ddof=1)\\n        boot[i] = sample.mean() / s if s > 0 else np.nan\\n    boot = boot[~np.isnan(boot)]\\n    if len(boot) == 0:\\n        return float(\\\"nan\\\"), float(\\\"nan\\\")\\n    return (float(np.percentile(boot, 100 * alpha / 2)),\\n            float(np.percentile(boot, 100 * (1 - alpha / 2))))\\n\\n\\ndef interpret_d(d: float) -> str:\\n    ad = abs(d)\\n    if ad < 0.2:\\n        return \\\"negligible\\\"\\n    elif ad < 0.5:\\n        return \\\"small\\\"\\n    elif ad < 0.8:\\n        return \\\"medium\\\"\\n    return \\\"large\\\"\\n\\n\\ndef midphase_null_rate(problems: List[dict]) -> Dict[str, Any]:\\n    total = len(problems)\\n    null_mid = sum(1 for p in problems if p.get(\\\"ii_mid\\\") is None)\\n    null_low = sum(1 for p in problems if p.get(\\\"ii_low\\\") is None)\\n    null_high = sum(1 for p in problems if p.get(\\\"ii_high\\\") is None)\\n    return {\\n        \\\"n_total\\\": total,\\n        \\\"null_high\\\": null_high,\\n        \\\"null_high_rate\\\": round(null_high / total, 4) if total > 0 else None,\\n        \\\"effective_n_high\\\": total - null_high,\\n        \\\"null_mid\\\": null_mid,\\n        \\\"null_mid_rate\\\": round(null_mid / total, 4) if total > 0 else None,\\n        \\\"effective_n_mid\\\": total - null_mid,\\n        \\\"null_low\\\": null_low,\\n        \\\"null_low_rate\\\": round(null_low / total, 4) if total > 0 else None,\\n        \\\"effective_n_low\\\": total - null_low,\\n    }\\n\\n\\ndef permutation_monotonicity_test(medians_by_sparsity: Dict[str, float],\\n                                   n_perm: int = N_PERM, seed: int = SEED) -> Dict[str, Any]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Test monotonic trend: Spearman rho + permutation test on gradient sum.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    ordered = sorted(medians_by_sparsity.items(), key=lambda x: SPARSITY_VALUES.get(x[0], 0))\\n    if len(ordered) < 3:\\n        return {\\\"error\\\": f\\\"need >=3 sparsity levels, got {len(ordered)}\\\"}\\n\\n    labels = [o[0] for o in ordered]\\n    vals = np.array([o[1] for o in ordered])\\n    ranks = np.arange(len(vals))\\n\\n    # Spearman rank correlation\\n    spearman_rho, spearman_p = stats.spearmanr(ranks, vals)\\n\\n    diffs_consecutive = np.diff(vals)\\n    n_increasing = int(np.sum(diffs_consecutive > 0))\\n    is_monotonic = bool(n_increasing == len(diffs_consecutive))\\n\\n    observed_stat = float(np.sum(diffs_consecutive))\\n\\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\\n    count = 0\\n    for _ in range(n_perm):\\n        perm_vals = rng.permutation(vals)\\n        perm_stat = float(np.sum(np.diff(perm_vals)))\\n        if perm_stat >= observed_stat:\\n            count += 1\\n    p = (count + 1) / (n_perm + 1)\\n\\n    return {\\n        \\\"sparsity_order\\\": labels,\\n        \\\"medians\\\": vals.tolist(),\\n        \\\"consecutive_diffs\\\": diffs_consecutive.tolist(),\\n        \\\"n_increasing_steps\\\": n_increasing,\\n        \\\"is_monotonic\\\": is_monotonic,\\n        \\\"spearman_rho\\\": float(spearman_rho),\\n        \\\"spearman_p\\\": float(spearman_p),\\n        \\\"sum_gradient_stat\\\": observed_stat,\\n        \\\"permutation_p\\\": float(p),\\n        \\\"n_perm\\\": n_perm,\\n    }\\n\\n\\ndef bonferroni(p: float, n_tests: int) -> float:\\n    return min(p * n_tests, 1.0)\\n\\n\\n# ── Per-condition analysis ────────────────────────────────────────────\\n\\ndef analyze_condition(problems: List[dict]) -> Dict[str, Any]:\\n    diffs = extract_diffs(problems, \\\"low\\\", \\\"high\\\")\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.44}}"}

List[dict], pa: str = \\\"low\\\", pb: str = \\\"high\\\") -> np.ndarray:\\n    diffs = []\\n    for p in problems:\\n        va, vb = p.get(f\\\"ii_{pa}\\\"), p.get(f\\\"ii_{pb}\\\")\\n        if va is not None and vb is not None:\\n            diffs.append(va - vb)\\n    return np.array(diffs)\\n\\n\\ndef bootstrap_median_ci(arr: np.ndarray, B: int = B_BOOT,\\n                        alpha: float = ALPHA, seed: int = SEED) -> Tuple[float, float]:\\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\\n    n = len(arr)\\n    medians = np.empty(B)\\n    for i in range(B):\\n        medians[i] = np.median(arr[rng.randint(0, n, size=n)])\\n    lo = float(np.percentile(medians, 100 * alpha / 2))\\n    hi = float(np.percentile(medians, 100 * (1 - alpha / 2)))\\n    return lo, hi\\n\\n\\ndef paired_cohens_dz(diffs: np.ndarray) -> float:\\n    if len(diffs) < 2:\\n        return float(\\\"nan\\\")\\n    s = np.std(diffs, ddof=1)\\n    if s == 0:\\n        return float(\\\"nan\\\")\\n    return float(np.mean(diffs) / s)\\n\\n\\ndef bootstrap_dz_ci(diffs: np.ndarray, B: int = B_BOOT,\\n                     alpha: float = ALPHA, seed: int = SEED) -> Tuple[float, float]:\\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\\n    n = len(diffs)\\n    boot = np.empty(B)\\n    for i in range(B):\\n        sample = diffs[rng.randint(0, n, size=n)]\\n        s = sample.std(ddof=1)\\n        boot[i] = sample.mean() / s if s > 0 else np.nan\\n    boot = boot[~np.isnan(boot)]\\n    if len(boot) == 0:\\n        return float(\\\"nan\\\"), float(\\\"nan\\\")\\n    return (float(np.percentile(boot, 100 * alpha / 2)),\\n            float(np.percentile(boot, 100 * (1 - alpha / 2))))\\n\\n\\ndef interpret_d(d: float) -> str:\\n    ad = abs(d)\\n    if ad < 0.2:\\n        return \\\"negligible\\\"\\n    elif ad < 0.5:\\n        return \\\"small\\\"\\n    elif ad < 0.8:\\n        return \\\"medium\\\"\\n    return \\\"large\\\"\\n\\n\\ndef midphase_null_rate(problems: List[dict]) -> Dict[str, Any]:\\n    total = len(problems)\\n    null_mid = sum(1 for p in problems if p.get(\\\"ii_mid\\\") is None)\\n    null_low = sum(1 for p in problems if p.get(\\\"ii_low\\\") is None)\\n    null_high = sum(1 for p in problems if p.get(\\\"ii_high\\\") is None)\\n    return {\\n        \\\"n_total\\\": total,\\n        \\\"null_high\\\": null_high,\\n        \\\"null_high_rate\\\": round(null_high / total, 4) if total > 0 else None,\\n        \\\"effective_n_high\\\": total - null_high,\\n        \\\"null_mid\\\": null_mid,\\n        \\\"null_mid_rate\\\": round(null_mid / total, 4) if total > 0 else None,\\n        \\\"effective_n_mid\\\": total - null_mid,\\n        \\\"null_low\\\": null_low,\\n        \\\"null_low_rate\\\": round(null_low / total, 4) if total > 0 else None,\\n        \\\"effective_n_low\\\": total - null_low,\\n    }\\n\\n\\ndef permutation_monotonicity_test(medians_by_sparsity: Dict[str, float],\\n                                   n_perm: int = N_PERM, seed: int = SEED) -> Dict[str, Any]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Test monotonic trend: Spearman rho + permutation test on gradient sum.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    ordered = sorted(medians_by_sparsity.items(), key=lambda x: SPARSITY_VALUES.get(x[0], 0))\\n    if len(ordered) < 3:\\n        return {\\\"error\\\": f\\\"need >=3 sparsity levels, got {len(ordered)}\\\"}\\n\\n    labels = [o[0] for o in ordered]\\n    vals = np.array([o[1] for o in ordered])\\n    ranks = np.arange(len(vals))\\n\\n    # Spearman rank correlation\\n    spearman_rho, spearman_p = stats.spearmanr(ranks, vals)\\n\\n    diffs_consecutive = np.diff(vals)\\n    n_increasing = int(np.sum(diffs_consecutive > 0))\\n    is_monotonic = bool(n_increasing == len(diffs_consecutive))\\n\\n    observed_stat = float(np.sum(diffs_consecutive))\\n\\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\\n    count = 0\\n    for _ in range(n_perm):\\n        perm_vals = rng.permutation(vals)\\n        perm_stat = float(np.sum(np.diff(perm_vals)))\\n        if perm_stat >= observed_stat:\\n            count += 1\\n    p = (count + 1) / (n_perm + 1)\\n\\n    return {\\n        \\\"sparsity_order\\\": labels,\\n        \\\"medians\\\": vals.tolist(),\\n        \\\"consecutive_diffs\\\": diffs_consecutive.tolist(),\\n        \\\"n_increasing_steps\\\": n_increasing,\\n        \\\"is_monotonic\\\": is_monotonic,\\n        \\\"spearman_rho\\\": float(spearman_rho),\\n        \\\"spearman_p\\\": float(spearman_p),\\n        \\\"sum_gradient_stat\\\": observed_stat,\\n        \\\"permutation_p\\\": float(p),\\n        \\\"n_perm\\\": n_perm,\\n    }\\n\\n\\ndef bonferroni(p: float, n_tests: int) -> float:\\n    return min(p * n_tests, 1.0)\\n\\n\\n# ── Per-condition analysis ────────────────────────────────────────────\\n\\ndef analyze_condition(problems: List[dict]) -> Dict[str, Any]:\\n    diffs = extract_diffs(problems,       "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nUnified n=500 analysis for phase-gated compound sparsity (PACS).\\n\\nAnalyses performed:\\n  1. Median II (low-high) + bootstrap 95% CI (B=10000, percentile method)\\n  2. Mid-phase null rate per condition\\n  3. Within-importance paired Cohen's d_z across sparsity levels (D059)\\n     - imp-vs-rand: Wilcoxon p + median_diff only, NO d_z\\n  4. Permutation monotonicity test across sparsity gradient\\n  5. Bonferroni correction on all reported p-values\\n\\nInput:  profiling_output_{n500_,}bf16_{ds,qw}_{imp,rand}_s{015..030}/profiling_results.json\\nOutput: analysis_output/n500_unified{_dryrun}.json\\n        analysis_output/n500_unified{_dryrun}.csv  (--csv)\\n        LaTeX table rows to stdout (--latex)\\n\\nConstraints:\\n  D054: median only, no mean\\n  D059: d_z within-importance only; imp-vs-rand gets Wilcoxon + median_diff\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport json\\nimport argparse\\nimport sys\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\nfrom typing import Optional, List, Dict, Tuple, Any\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\nfrom scipy import stats\\n\\nBASE = Path(\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/pacs\\\")\\n\\nMODEL_MAP = {\\\"deepseek\\\": \\\"ds\\\", \\\"qwen\\\": \\\"qw\\\"}\\nMASKING_TYPES = [\\\"imp\\\", \\\"rand\\\"]\\nSPARSITY_LABELS = [\\\"s015\\\", \\\"s020\\\", \\\"s025\\\", \\\"s030\\\"]\\nSPARSITY_VALUES = {\\\"s015\\\": 0.15, \\\"s020\\\": 0.20, \\\"s025\\\": 0.25, \\\"s030\\\": 0.30}\\nSPARSITY_KEY_MAP = {\\\"s015\\\": \\\"0.15\\\", \\\"s020\\\": \\\"0.2\\\", \\\"s025\\\": \\\"0.25\\\", \\\"s030\\\": \\\"0.3\\\"}\\n\\nWITHIN_IMP_PAIRS = [\\n    (\\\"s015\\\", \\\"s020\\\"),\\n    (\\\"s020\\\", \\\"s025\\\"),\\n    (\\\"s025\\\", \\\"s030\\\"),\\n    (\\\"s015\\\", \\\"s030\\\"),\\n]\\n\\nB_BOOT = 10000\\nSEED = 42\\nALPHA = 0.05\\nN_PERM = 5000\\n\\n\\n# ── Path / IO ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\\n\\ndef dir_name(model_short: str, masking: str, slabel: str, n500: bool = True) -> str:\\n    prefix = \\\"n500_\\\" if n500 else \\\"\\\"\\n    return f\\\"profiling_output_{prefix}bf16_{model_short}_{masking}_{slabel}\\\"\\n\\n\\ndef load_problems(dirpath: Path) -> Optional[List[dict]]:\\n    fpath = dirpath / \\\"profiling_results.json\\\"\\n    if not fpath.exists():\\n        return None\\n    with open(fpath) as f:\\n        data = json.load(f)\\n    ppi = data.get(\\\"per_problem_ii\\\", {})\\n    keys = list(ppi.keys())\\n    if not keys:\\n        return None\\n    return ppi[keys[0]]\\n\\n\\n# ── Statistics ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\\n\\ndef extract_diffs(problems: List[dict], pa: str = \\\"low\\\", pb: str = \\\"high\\\") -> np.ndarray:\\n    diffs = []\\n    for p in problems:\\n        va, vb = p.get(f\\\"ii_{pa}\\\"), p.get(f\\\"ii_{pb}\\\")\\n        if va is not None and vb is not None:\\n            diffs.append(va - vb)\\n    return np.array(diffs)\\n\\n\\ndef bootstrap_median_ci(arr: np.ndarray, B: int = B_BOOT,\\n                        alpha: float = ALPHA, seed: int = SEED) -> Tuple[float, float]:\\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\\n    n = len(arr)\\n    medians = np.empty(B)\\n    for i in range(B):\\n        medians[i] = np.median(arr[rng.randint(0, n, size=n)])\\n    lo = float(np.percentile(medians, 100 * alpha / 2))\\n    hi = float(np.percentile(medians, 100 * (1 - alpha / 2)))\\n    return lo, hi\\n\\n\\ndef paired_cohens_dz(diffs: np.ndarray) -> float:\\n    if len(diffs) < 2:\\n        return float(\\\"nan\\\")\\n    s = np.std(diffs, ddof=1)\\n    if s == 0:\\n        return float(\\\"nan\\\")\\n    return float(np.mean(diffs) / s)\\n\\n\\ndef bootstrap_dz_ci(diffs: np.ndarray, B: int = B_BOOT,\\n                     alpha: float = ALPHA, seed: int = SEED) -> Tuple[float, float]:\\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\\n    n = len(diffs)\\n    boot = np.empty(B)\\n    for i in range(B):\\n        sample = diffs[rng.randint(0, n, size=n)]\\n        s = sample.std(ddof=1)\\n        boot[i] = sample.mean() / s if s > 0 else np.nan\\n    boot = boot[~np.isnan(boot)]\\n    if len(boot) == 0:\\n        return float(\\\"nan\\\"), float(\\\"nan\\\")\\n    return (float(np.percentile(boot, 100 * alpha / 2)),\\n            float(np.percentile(boot, 100 * (1 - alpha / 2))))\\n\\n\\ndef interpret_d(d: float) -> str:\\n    ad = abs(d)\\n    if ad < 0.2:\\n        return \\\"negligible\\\"\\n    elif ad < 0.5:\\n        return \\\"small\\\"\\n    elif ad < 0.8:\\n        return \\\"medium\\\"\\n    return \\\"large\\\"\\n\\n\\ndef midphase_null_rate(problems: Imp-vs-rand comparisons: Wilcoxon + median_diff (D059: no d_z)\\n    imp_vs_rand = {}\\n    for sl in SPARSITY_LABELS:\\n        key_imp = f\\\"imp_{sl}\\\"\\n        key_rand = f\\\"rand_{sl}\\\"\\n        if key_imp not in all_problems or key_rand not in all_problems:\\n            imp_vs_rand[sl] = {\\\"error\\\": \\\"missing data\\\"}\\n            continue\\n\\n        diffs_imp = extract_diffs(all_problems[key_imp], \\\"low\\\", \\\"high\\\")\\n        diffs_rand = extract_diffs(all_problems[key_rand], \\\"low\\\", \\\"high\\\")\\n        n_paired = min(len(diffs_imp), len(diffs_rand))\\n        if n_paired < 5:\\n            imp_vs_rand[sl] = {\\\"error\\\": f\\\"too few paired: {n_paired}\\\"}\\n            continue\\n\\n        paired_diffs = diffs_imp[:n_paired] - diffs_rand[:n_paired]\\n        w_stat, w_p = stats.wilcoxon(paired_diffs, alternative=\\\"two-sided\\\")\\n        imp_vs_rand[sl] = {\\n            \\\"median_diff\\\": float(np.median(paired_diffs)),\\n            \\\"wilcoxon_stat\\\": float(w_stat),\\n            \\\"wilcoxon_p\\\": float(w_p),\\n            \\\"n_paired\\\": n_paired,\\n        }\\n\\n    # 5. Permutation monotonicity test\\n    dose_imp = {}\\n    dose_rand = {}\\n    for masking, dose_dict in [(\\\"imp\\\", dose_imp), (\\\"rand\\\", dose_rand)]:\\n        medians = {}\\n        for sl in SPARSITY_LABELS:\\n            key = f\\\"{masking}_{sl}\\\"\\n            if key in cond_out and \\\"error\\\" not in cond_out[key]:\\n                medians[sl] = cond_out[key][\\\"median_ii_diff\\\"]\\n        if len(medians) >= 3:\\n            dose_dict.update(permutation_monotonicity_test(medians))\\n        else:\\n            dose_dict[\\\"error\\\"] = f\\\"need >=3 levels, got {len(medians)}\\\"\\n\\n    # 6.       "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nUnified n=500 analysis for phase-gated compound sparsity (PACS).\\n\\nAnalyses performed:\\n  1. Median II (low-high) + bootstrap 95% CI (B=10000, percentile method)\\n  2. Mid-phase null rate per condition\\n  3. Within-importance paired Cohen's d_z across sparsity levels (D059)\\n     - imp-vs-rand: Wilcoxon p + median_diff only, NO d_z\\n  4. Permutation monotonicity test across sparsity gradient\\n  5. Bonferroni correction on all reported p-values\\n\\nInput:  profiling_output_{n500_,}bf16_{ds,qw}_{imp,rand}_s{015..030}/profiling_results.json\\nOutput: analysis_output/n500_unified{_dryrun}.json\\n        analysis_output/n500_unified{_dryrun}.csv  (--csv)\\n        LaTeX table rows to stdout (--latex)\\n\\nConstraints:\\n  D054: median only, no mean\\n  D059: d_z within-importance only; imp-vs-rand gets Wilcoxon + median_diff\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport json\\nimport argparse\\nimport sys\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\nfrom typing import Optional, List, Dict, Tuple, Any\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\nfrom scipy import stats\\n\\nBASE = Path(\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/pacs\\\")\\n\\nMODEL_MAP = {\\\"deepseek\\\": \\\"ds\\\", \\\"qwen\\\": \\\"qw\\\"}\\nMASKING_TYPES = [\\\"imp\\\", \\\"rand\\\"]\\nSPARSITY_LABELS = [\\\"s015\\\", \\\"s020\\\", \\\"s025\\\", \\\"s030\\\"]\\nSPARSITY_VALUES = {\\\"s015\\\": 0.15, \\\"s020\\\": 0.20, \\\"s025\\\": 0.25, \\\"s030\\\": 0.30}\\nSPARSITY_KEY_MAP = {\\\"s015\\\": \\\"0.15\\\", \\\"s020\\\": \\\"0.2\\\", \\\"s025\\\": \\\"0.25\\\", \\\"s030\\\": \\\"0.3\\\"}\\n\\nWITHIN_IMP_PAIRS = [\\n    (\\\"s015\\\", \\\"s020\\\"),\\n    (\\\"s020\\\", \\\"s025\\\"),\\n    (\\\"s025\\\", \\\"s030\\\"),\\n    (\\\"s015\\\", \\\"s030\\\"),\\n]\\n\\nB_BOOT = 10000\\nSEED = 42\\nALPHA = 0.05\\nN_PERM = 5000\\n\\n\\n# ── Path / IO ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\\n\\ndef dir_name(model_short: str, masking: str, slabel: str, n500: bool = True) -> str:\\n    prefix = \\\"n500_\\\" if n500 else \\\"\\\"\\n    return f\\\"profiling_output_{prefix}bf16_{model_short}_{masking}_{slabel}\\\"\\n\\n\\ndef load_problems(dirpath: Path) -> Optional[List[dict]]:\\n    fpath = dirpath / \\\"profiling_results.json\\\"\\n    if not fpath.exists():\\n        return None\\n    with open(fpath) as f:\\n        data = json.load(f)\\n    ppi = data.get(\\\"per_problem_ii\\\", {})\\n    keys = list(ppi.keys())\\n    if not keys:\\n        return None\\n    return ppi[keys[0]]\\n\\n\\n# ── Statistics ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\\n\\ndef extract_diffs(problems: List[dict], pa: str = \\\"low\\\", pb: str = \\\"high\\\") -> np.ndarray:\\n    diffs = []\\n    for p in problems:\\n        va, vb = p.get(f\\\"ii_{pa}\\\"), p.get(f\\\"ii_{pb}\\\")\\n        if va is not None and vb is not None:\\n            diffs.append(va - vb)\\n    return np.array(diffs)\\n\\n\\ndef bootstrap_median_ci(arr: np.ndarray, B: int = B_BOOT,\\n                        alpha: float = ALPHA, seed: int = SEED) -> Tuple[float, float]:\\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\\n    n = len(arr)\\n    medians = np.empty(B)\\n    for i in range(B):\\n        medians[i] = np.median(arr[rng.randint(0, n, size=n)])\\n    lo = float(np.percentile(medians, 100 * alpha / 2))\\n    hi = float(np.percentile(medians, 100 * (1 - alpha / 2)))\\n    return lo, hi\\n\\n\\ndef paired_cohens_dz(diffs: np.ndarray) -> float:\\n    if len(diffs) < 2:\\n        return float(\\\"nan\\\")\\n    s = np.std(diffs, ddof=1)\\n    if s == 0:\\n        return float(\\\"nan\\\")\\n boot[i] = sample.mean() / s if s > 0 else np.nan\\n    boot = boot[~np.isnan(boot)]\\n    if len(boot) == 0:\\n        return float(\\\"nan\\\"), float(\\\"nan\\\")\\n    return \\\"s020\\\"),\\n    (\\\"s020\\\", \\\"s025\\\"),\\n    (\\\"s025\\\", \\\"s030\\\"),\\n    (\\\"s015\\\", \\\"s030\\\"),\\n]\\n\\nB_BOOT = 10000\\nSEED = 42\\nALPHA = 0.05\\nN_PERM = 5000\\n\\n\\n# \\\"profiling_results.json\\\"\\n    if not fpath.exists():\\n        return None\\n    with open(fpath) as f:\\n        data = json.load(f)\\n    ppi = data.get(\\\"per_problem_ii\\\", {})\\n    keys = list(ppi.keys())\\n    if not keys:\\n        return None\\n    return ppi[keys[0]]\\n\\n\\n# ── Statistics ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\\n\\ndef extract_diffs(problems: List[dict], pa: str = \\\"low\\\", pb: str = \\\"high\\\") -> np.ndarray:\\n    diffs = []\\n    for p in problems:\\n        va, vb = p.get(f\\\"ii_{pa}\\\"), p.get(f\\\"ii_{pb}\\\")\\n        if va is not None and vb is not None:\\n            diffs.append(va - vb)\\n    return np.array(diffs)\\n\\n\\ndef bootstrap_median_ci(arr: np.ndarray, B: int = B_BOOT,\\n                        alpha: float = ALPHA, seed: int = SEED) -> Tuple[float, float]:\\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\\n    n = len(arr)\\n    medians = np.empty(B)\\n    for i in range(B):\\n        medians[i] = np.median(arr[rng.randint(0, n, size=n)])\\n    lo = float(np.percentile(medians, 100 * alpha / 2))\\n    hi = float(np.percentile(medians, 100 * (1 - alpha / 2)))\\n    return lo, hi\\n\\n\\ndef paired_cohens_dz(diffs: np.ndarray) -> float:\\n    if len(diffs) < 2:\\n        return float(\\\"nan\\\")\\n    s = np.std(diffs, ddof=1)\\n    if s == 0:\\n        return float(\\\"nan\\\")\\n    return float(np.mean(diffs) / s)\\n\\n\\ndef bootstrap_dz_ci(diffs: np.ndarray, B: int = B_BOOT,\\n                     alpha: float = ALPHA, seed: int = SEED) -> Tuple[float, float]:\\n    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)\\n    n = len(diffs)\\n    boot = np.empty(B)\\n    for i in range(B):\\n        sample = diffs[rng.randint(0, n, size=n)]\\n        s = sample.std(ddof=1)\\n        boot[i] = sample.mean() / s if s > 0 else np.nan\\n    boot = boot[~np.isnan(boot)]\\n    if len(boot) == 0:\\n        return float(\\\"nan\\\"), float(\\\"nan\\\")\\n    return (float(np.percentile(boot, 100 * alpha / 2)),\\n            float(np.percentile(boot, 100 * (1 - alpha / 2))))\\n\\n\\ndef interpret_d(d: float) -> str:\\n    ad = abs(d)\\n    if ad < 0.2:\\n        return \\\"negligible\\\"\\n    elif ad < 0.5:\\n        return \\\"small\\\"\\n    elif ad < 0.8:\\n        return \\\"medium\\\"\\n    return \\\"large\\\"\\n\\n\\ndef midphase_null_rate(problems: List[dict]) -> Dict[str, Any]:\\n    total = len(problems)\\n    null_mid = sum(1 for p in problems if p.get(\\\"ii_mid\\\") is None)\\n    null_low = sum(1 for p in problems if p.get(\\\"ii_low\\\") is None)\\n    null_high = sum(1 for p in problems if p.get(\\\"ii_high\\\") is None)\\n    return {\\n        \\\"n_total\\\": total,\\n        \\\"null_high\\\": null_high,\\n        \\\"null_high_rate\\\": round(null_high / total, 4) if total > 0 else None,\\n        \\\"effective_n_high\\\": total - null_high,\\n        \\\"null_mid\\\": null_mid,\\n                              n_perm: int = N_PERM, seed: int = SEED) -> Dict[str, Any]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Test monotonic trend: Spearman rho + permutation test on gradient sum.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    ordered = sorted(medians_by_sparsity.items(), key=lambda x: SPARSITY_VALUES.get(x[0], 0))\\n    if len(ordered) < 3:\\n        return {\\\"error\\\": f\\\"need >=3 sparsity levels, got {len(ordered)}\\\"}\\n\\n    labels = [o[0] for o in ordered]\\n    vals = np.array([o[1] for o in ordered])\\n    ranks = np.arange(len(vals))\\n\\n    # Spearman rank correlation\\n    spearman_rho, spearman_p = stats.spearmanr(ranks, vals)\\n\\n    diffs_consecutive = np.diff(vals)\\n    n_increasing = int(np.sum(diffs_consecutive > 0))\\n    is_monotonic = bool(n_increasing == Add new helper functions after interpret_d()\n# ============================================================\nafter_interpret_d = '''def interpret_d(d: float) -> str:\n    ad = abs(d)\n    if ad < 0.2:\n        return \"negligible\"\n    elif ad < 0.5:\n        return \"small\"\n    elif ad < 0.8:\n        return \"medium\"\n    return \"large\"'''\n\nnew_helpers = after_interpret_d + '''\n\n\ndef sig_stars(p: float) -> str:\n    if p < 0.001:\n        return \"***\"\n    if p < 0.01:\n        return \"**\"\n    if p < 0.05:\n        return \"*\"\n    return \"ns\"\n\n\ndef matched_pairs_rank_biserial(diffs: np.ndarray) -> float:\n    nonzero = diffs[diffs != 0]\n    n = len(nonzero)\n    if n < 2:\n        return float(\"nan\")\n    ranks = stats.rankdata(np.abs(nonzero))\n    w_plus = float(np.sum(ranks[nonzero > 0]))\n    w_minus = float(np.sum(ranks[nonzero < 0]))\n    denom = n * (n + 1) / 2.0\n    return float((w_plus - w_minus) / denom)\n\n\ndef dz_is_reliable(dz_ci: list, slabel_a: str, slabel_b: str) -> bool:\n    ci_lo, ci_hi = dz_ci\n    if any(np.isnan(x) for x in [ci_lo, ci_hi]):\n        return False\n    ci_width = ci_hi - ci_lo\n    if ci_width > 0.5:\n        return False\n    high_sparsity = slabel_a in (\"s025\", \"s030\") or slabel_b in (\"s025\", \"s030\")\n    if high_sparsity:\n        mid = (ci_lo + ci_hi) / 2.0\n        upper = ci_hi - mid\n        lower = mid - ci_lo\n        if lower > 0 and upper / lower > 3.0:\n            return False\n        if upper > 0 and lower / upper > 3.0:\n            return False\n    return True'''\n\ncode = code.replace(after_interpret_d, new_helpers)\n\n# ============================================================\n# 3. Add sig_stars to analyze_condition output\n# ============================================================\nold_condition_return = \"\"\"    return {\n        \"n_valid\": n_valid,\n        \"median_ii_diff\": med,\n        \"median_ii_diff_ci\": [ci_lo, ci_hi],\n        \"ci_excludes_zero\": not crosses_zero,\n        \"wilcoxon_stat\": float(wilcoxon_stat),\n        \"wilcoxon_p\": float(wilcoxon_p),\n        \"per_phase_median\": per_phase,\n        \"midphase_null\": null_info,\n    }\"\"\"\n\nnew_condition_return = \"\"\"    p_val = float(wilcoxon_p)\n    return {\n        \"n_valid\": n_valid,\n        \"median_ii_diff\": med,\n        \"median_ii_diff_ci\": [ci_lo, ci_hi],\n        \"ci_excludes_zero\": not crosses_zero,\n        \"significance\": sig_stars(p_val),\n        \"wilcoxon_stat\": float(wilcoxon_stat),\n        \"wilcoxon_p\": p_val,\n        \"per_phase_median\": per_phase,\n        \"midphase_null\": null_info,\n    }\"\"\"\n\ncode = code.replace(old_condition_return, new_condition_return)\n\n# ============================================================\n# 4. Add rank_biserial to imp_vs_rand\n# ============================================================\nold_ivr = \"\"\"        imp_vs_rand[sl] = {\n            \"median_diff\": float(np.median(paired_diffs)),\n            \"wilcoxon_stat\": float(w_stat),\n            \"wilcoxon_p\": float(w_p),\n            \"n_paired\": n_paired,\n        }\n\n    # 5. Permutation monotonicity test\"\"\"\n\nnew_ivr = \"\"\"        r_rb_ivr = matched_pairs_rank_biserial(paired_diffs)\n        imp_vs_rand[sl] = {\n            \"median_diff\": float(np.median(paired_diffs)),\n            \"rank_biserial_r\": r_rb_ivr,\n            \"wilcoxon_stat\": float(w_stat),\n            \"wilcoxon_p\": float(w_p),\n            \"n_paired\": n_paired,\n        }\n\n    # 5.
